# Data Folder Usage

Every file under the repository `Data/` folder, checked against the data the notebooks read (`DATA_INVENTORY.csv`). Generated by `Tutorials/_tools/data_usage.py` (kept locally in `helper_functions/`, not in the repository); the full file list is `Tutorials/DATA_USAGE.csv`.

- Files: **52,273** (526.4 GB)
- Used by at least one notebook: **32,500** (306.8 GB)
- Not used by any notebook: **19,773** (219.7 GB)

*Used* also counts companion files of a used file (shapefile `.dbf`/`.shx`/`.prj`, ENVI `.hdr`, `.aux.xml`) and files inside folders a notebook reads as a whole (band folders, `.zarr`, `.gdb`, `.SAFE`). The check reads the code without running it, so treat *not used* as a strong hint, and look before deleting anything.

## Contents

- [Summary by data folder](#summary-by-data-folder)
- [Files not used by any notebook](#files-not-used-by-any-notebook)
